This week's cover story

One page. One fight. Read it and you're ahead of half the room.

Does the city clerk certify the referendum — and does a judge let it stand?

Organizers turned in roughly 3,200 signatures; 2,739 valid Cheyenne-registered 2024 voters are required under the Title 22 referendum statute. The city attorney is simultaneously asking a district court whether annexations must be fought under W.S. 15-1-409 instead. Until both questions resolve, Ordinance No. 4687 — the largest annexation in city history — sits in suspension's doorway.

WATCH FOR: Clerk's final count; declaratory-judgment filing; any motion invoking W.S. 22-23-1005/1007.

The saga so far

The 2026 chronology, each entry pinned to published reporting. Click [record]. Check the work.

  • 2026-01-12Administrative warrant ordinance advances 7-3. A resident tells the council a municipal judge can't issue the city's own search warrants. [record]
  • 2026-01-13County commissioners unanimously approve Project Jade — 1.8 GW now, potentially 10 GW, the largest AI campus proposal in the country. [record]
  • 2026-02-09Warrant ordinance passes 7-3, but 'discover' and 'determine' are struck. 'Verify' survives. Public comment moved the text. [record]
  • 2026-02-28BOPU routine sampling finds Cupriavidus gilardii in the reclaimed water system. Public won't be told for ~4 months. [record]
  • 2026-03-24BOPU revokes Goat Systems LLC's discharge privileges — the contractor on Meta's $800M data center. [record]
  • 2026-04-14Iron Guard's 800-unit, up-to-5,600-worker 'man camp' plan surfaces — bigger than 84 Wyoming towns. [record]
  • 2026-05-2612-month data-center moratorium fails 8-1 after four hours of testimony. Moody is the only yes. [record]
  • 2026-06-16Iron Guard withdraws the man camp after weeks of backlash; county keeps the contact list in case it returns. [record]
  • 2026-06-22Council advances Microsoft's 3,500-acre annexation. 'You are granting municipal jurisdiction to a 3,500-acre ghost project,' a resident testifies. 'Premeditated regulatory arbitrage.' [record]
  • 2026-07-08BOPU confirms the rare bacterium was traced to Meta's contractor; city bans all data-center fill-and-flush discharges. Rep. Hageman writes Mark Zuckerberg. [record]
  • 2026-07-13Ordinance No. 4687 passes 6-3 just before 2 a.m. — the largest annexation in Cheyenne's history. Rep. Ann Lucas warns it is 'likely irreversible.' [record]
  • 2026-08-07Volunteers file the referendum petition: about 3,200 signatures against a 2,739-signature requirement. [record]
  • 2026-08-10After an unagendized post-executive-session vote, the council directs the city attorney to ask a court whether the referendum process even applies. Moody dissents. [record]
  • 2026-08-14Secretary of State Chuck Gray tells the council: don't 'abuse legal processes and procedures to bog down Cheyenne residents' constitutional rights under the initiative process.' [record]
  • 2026-08-18Primary sets November: Kallas v. Brand (Ward 1, open), Rose v. Storey-Huylar (Ward 2, open), Esquibel v. Bryant (Ward 3). [record]
  • 2026-08-18Same week, Finance Committee hears the $186,500 roundabout re-study. 'You are quite literally paying a premium consultant to study a study of your own failed design.' It passes the full council Aug. 25 anyway. [record]
  • 2026-09-01County commissioners approve Vantage's 'Project Latigo' zone change — 1,835 acres, its own gas plant, no public commenters at the planning stage. [record]
  • 2026-11-03General election. Three council seats — and, if the clerk certifies and the court allows, the annexation referendum.

🦸 NEW HEROES WANTED

Every power on the Roster started as one citizen, one mic, three minutes. Read the statute. Quote the record. Sign your name. — send a signal · join the repo

Method. This site is generated from a local ledger of public pages and documents fetched politely (robots.txt honored, 3-second minimum between requests, no logins, no paywall circumvention). Claims without a linked record do not get printed.

  • AI summaries here are extractive and method-labeled, or absent. The record is the text.
